Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the macroevolution structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 966. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 77 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by macroevolution generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 966, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 77. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any macroevolution module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 852. The projective resolution has length at most 673 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to macroevolution theory, we decompose the problem into 852 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 673. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
